# ULX3S project catalogue

Generated by `.claude/skills/review-gateware-project/gen_catalogue.py` from `.claude/memory/catalogue.tsv`. Do not edit by hand.

**Toolchain**: `open` = yosys + nextpnr-ecp5 + ecppack (incl. via apio/LiteX/ghdl plugin), `diamond` = Lattice Diamond, `both` = both build paths exist. **License** `none found` = no license file/header found (all rights reserved by default; ask the author before reusing). **Fork** column: which copy to prefer when several clones exist.

## At a glance

- 293 repositories catalogued.
- Gateware toolchains: open 216, both 23, diamond 21, n/a 8, unknown 1.
- 150 repositories have no license found.
- 137 repositories have some detected testbench/simulation setup (heuristic scan; see "Testbenches and simulation" below).

## Testbenches and simulation

Heuristic scan (`.claude/skills/review-gateware-project/scan_tests.sh`, 2026-09-27) over each shallow clone for test/sim directories (`tb/`, `test/`, `sim/`, ...), testbench-named files (`*_tb.v`, `tb_*.vhd`, `*_test.py`, ...) and simulator mentions (iverilog, Verilator, GHDL, cocotb, VUnit) in build scripts. It is a name/path heuristic, not proof the tests run or pass — verify before relying on one. Repos not listed here had nothing matching.

## Functions and reusable blocks

Per repo: function tags, where the ULX3S build lives, the constraint file, reusable cores, tests, and notes.

### acairncross__clash-ulx3s-examples

- **Reuse**: src/RAM.hs, src/DVI.hs, src/StackMachine.hs, src/UART.hs -- reusable Clash building blocks
- **Tests**: none found
- **Notes**: Five separate top modules built as separate bitstreams (stackMachine, counter, ram, dvi, snake)
